## Phase 3d: the dig read in ms, the wait by its parts, at the fly-through's pace

Code at `1d94e563`; a reading, no mechanism. The bake thread tells each step when it was free
(its start, or the end of the previous step's hand-in to the outbox) and when it took its
message (`thread::Picked`); `bake_step` hands back its start, its ms to the end of `boxes`
(the stages `setup` to `boxes`, laps of one clock in order), its return and the snapshot's end
(`link::StepTimes`). The edit's frame splits the time from the instant before its send to the
take's return into six parts (`link::DigWaitParts`): `rest` (the thread not free at the send),
`pickup`, `boxes`, `step_rest`, `snapshot` (`Books::of`; the dig trace under `trace_digs`),
`handback`. `Frame::bake_frame_wait_parts` reads the last edit's frame's parts and issue lap.
The timeline's dig rows gain an `ms` row (held and ring 1 in ms from the edit's frame's start
to the end of the frame that read them, the mean frame over the edit's frame to ring 1's, the
parts, the issue and apply laps, the call); the fly-through's teardown prints `bake thread dig
wait:` and `bake dig call:` per state (while in view waits, after), from the dig frames
recorded in `step_bake_queue`.

### The done-whens

| done-when | reading |
|---|---|
| (1) `the_waits_parts_sum_to_the_wait` | passes, 0.06 s (debug): the step before the dig's held 20 ms, the box sent 0.053 ms into the hold; wait 27.586 ms, parts rest 20.089 pickup 0.000 boxes 5.256 step_rest 2.204 snapshot 0.016 handback 0.012, sum 27.578 |
| (2) the timelines | 32 runs, below |
| (3) the fly-through | 6 runs, below |

## Phase 4c2: the bake thread's table slots

**What landed.** The queue's reserve is the last 64 table rows (`QUEUE_RESERVE`); the one-shot
loop takes the first 192 (`LOOP_SLOTS`, `const` assertions that the two do not meet and that
both counts lie in the reserve). `JobLoop` keeps the rows it opens into (`rows`, `set_rows`): a
slot freed outside them does not return to the free list, and a new range adds the rows no job
holds behind those already free. Lockstep and the pre-roll open into the last 16
(`QUEUE_SLOTS`, the loop made with `queue_rows(16)`, its free order as before); the listing
pool's start sets the last `THREAD_SLOTS` (32, the reading below), its stop the last 16 again.
`Frame::set_bake_slots` (fly-through `--bake-slots N`, 16 to 64; a `NOT_SETTINGS` row) sets the
count the thread's next start takes. The `bake queue slice:` line gains `steps neither blocked
nor sent I` (since the start) and `jobs open at the draw p50 J max M` (the steps that sent a
submit since the last line; `QueueStats::{idle_steps, open_at_draw}`).
Rig: `live/p4c2-batch.sh` (env `PASSES`, `SLOTS`, `RENOIR=N`), parser `live/p4c2-parse.py`,
the rest `live/p4c2-rest.sh`; binaries `live/bin/p4c2/{flythrough,gpu_outdoor}` (release, this
phase's tree; the RTX slot runs took the binary built with the default at 64 and every run
named its count with `--bake-slots`); archive `live/p4c2-tests.tar.zst`.

## Architect's ruling after Phase 4c2 (2026-11-26)

A code and log reading at `a338f8c7`; nothing was built or run. The rulings are in the plan
("Amended 2026-11-26 (architect, after Phase 4c2)"); this is what they stand on. Paths are
under `crates/svet-render/src/` unless they start with `apps/` or `live/`.

### 1. What the table's fed and submits a frame count (code, then the rows)

- **The per-second line counts frames, not submits.** `QueueRun::note_step`
  (`apps/flythrough/src/run/queue.rs` 213 to 216) pushes `st.ms` once for a frame whose
  stats read any sample back. That frame's stats are the sum over the steps it took
  (`ThreadLink::absorb`, `bake/link.rs` 383 to 386; `QueueStats::after`,
  `bake/queue/books.rs` 176 to 194: `samples`, `ms`, `read_submits`, `submitted` summed), and
  a step's own `ms` is the sum over the read-backs its message carried (`submit`,
  `bake/queue/step.rs` 458 to 460 and 517 to 520; `collect`'s loop, `bake/job_loop.rs` 589).
  `live/p4c-parse.py` takes that count (`over N submits`) as the submits and the line's p50
  as the drawn share: submits a frame is `sum(n) / frames`, fed is `sum(n * p50 / granted) /
  frames`.
- **The plans sent, from the slice line's own counters** (`plans stopped at the cap`, `at the
  batch`, `out of rounds` summed: `plan_stops` is raised once a dispatch that sent,
  `bake/job_loop.rs` 443 to 450), deltas over the seconds whose slice line reads in view
  waiting, beside the steps, the frames the per-second line counted, and the samples read
  back (the slice line's `of D drawn`) a frame:

| run | frames | steps | plans sent | frames counted as a submit | not blocked, not sent | blocked | samples read back a frame |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| s16-1 | 660 | 470 | 437 | 371 | 33 | 0 | 359 000 (over 720) |
| s16-2 | 1 980 | 1 563 | 1 509 | 1 272 | 53 | 1 | 426 000 |
| s32-1 | 1 920 | 1 252 | 1 252 | 1 015 | 0 | 0 | 440 000 |
| s32-2 | 1 860 | 1 212 | 1 212 | 982 | 0 | 0 | 432 000 |
| s48-1 | 1 920 | 1 262 | 1 262 | 1 016 | 0 | 0 | 437 000 |
| s48-2 | 1 800 | 1 217 | 1 217 | 973 | 0 | 0 | 457 000 |
| s64-1 | 1 920 | 1 249 | 1 248 | 1 018 | 0 | 1 | 437 000 |
| s64-2 | 1 920 | 1 253 | 1 253 | 1 011 | 0 | 0 | 437 000 |
| s32 RENOIR | 360 | 360 | 360 | 360 | 0 | 0 | |

  The frames are the per-second lines' own, so they differ from the table of (6) by a
  second at each end. s16-1's pre-roll (381 steps, 2 107 chunks) left no coarse block in
  view (`coarse 0` on every line); the other seven left 31 (`coarse 31`), which wait 9 to
  11 s after the chunks with no record have landed. That is why its view ends at 6.9 s: it
  had no in-view refinement, it did not land them sooner.
- **The cap in samples is not printed.** The per-second price on those lines reads 0.14 to
  0.53 ms a dispatch plus 1.7 to 2.2 ns, which prices 1.5 ms at about 630 000 to 650 000
  samples in the median second (s16-2 0.26 + 1.9, s32-1 0.20 + 2.0, s64-2 0.43 + 1.7).
  Against it the samples read back a frame are 0.66 at 16 (run 2) and 0.66 to 0.70 at 32 to
  64 (one run, s48-2, 0.72); by the plans sent, 0.65 a frame at 32 to 64, each stopped at the cap but 12 to 20 a
  run. **Both are derivations**; the line of Phase 4d reads the figure.

### 2. What a step does that grows (code)

- **One submit a step, at most:** `submit` calls `dispatch` once, behind `in_flight() <
  bound` (`bake/queue/step.rs` 506 to 511); `in_flight_bound` is the thread's depth
  (`bake/queue/lister.rs` 203 to 209). A step that neither sent nor was blocked is counted
  at 526.
- **By the entries of a submit** (a round is `QUEUE_ROUND`, 64 samples, `bake/queue/mod.rs`
  133; a cap of about 650 000 samples is about 10 000 entries): `fill_entries` builds an
  input row a round (`bake/job_loop.rs` 550 to 566, the push at 561); `collect` reads a row
  a round back into its face (613 to 669).
- **By the jobs open and their faces, whatever they have left to plan:** `plan_rounds` calls
  `JobCtx::plan` for every open job (489 to 490), which walks the job's live faces and
  pushes a pair for each while the submit has room (926 to 945), a job queued whole
  included (its faces stay live until their read-back marks them done); `fill_entries`
  walks those pairs again; `close_ended` re-filters every open job's live list once a
  read-back (724 to 727, called at 704); `samples_left` sums over every face state of
  every open job (329 to 343) and is read three times a step (`projection`,
  `bake/queue/lister.rs` 240 to 242, from `bake/queue/step.rs` 319 and
  `bake/queue/open.rs` 64; and `step.rs` 504).
- **By the residency:** the snapshot (`Books::of`, `bake/queue/books.rs` 58 to 103), made
  after `step_end` and before the entry is handed over (`bake/link.rs` 228 to 229), so it
  is in the thread's cycle and outside the stage table.
- **The thread's loop** (`bake/thread.rs` 112 to 143): take the message or wait for one,
  the step, the outbox's lock, then `free`. Nothing prints the time from one `took` to
  the next.

## Phase 3f: the edit's frame waits for the step's lists, not for its books

Commits: `519a2255` (`THREAD_SLOTS` 16, the knob's default in `docs/flythrough.md`; the
Phase 4 row's 4c2 cell `fd62b667`), `0c88a0c3` (the fix), `1b744e32` (the fly-through's dig
frame with the thread on). The bake thread's step hands its ops, lists and stats to the
outbox when it returns (`thread::Hand`, `link::Made::Step`), then makes the books' snapshot
and hands it in as its own entry (`Made::Books`, with the snapshot's ms). The render side
keeps the newest books taken (`absorb`), the newest lists' frame (`ThreadLink::lists_frame`)
and answers the mailbox up to the older of the lists applied and the books held
(`render_frame`). `take_waiting` ends on the dig's step entry; where that step's queue traced
digs (`Stepped::trace`) it ends on the books entry after it, and the `snapshot` part is then
the snapshot's span (0 otherwise). The teardown's `frames: bake thread snapshot:` line ends
with the snapshots taken, p50, p99 and max (`Frame::bake_snapshot_ms`). A test hook before the
snapshot (`BakeSide::books_hook`). Rigs: `live/p3f-build.sh`, `live/p3f-batch.sh`,
`live/p3f-fly2.sh`; binaries `live/bin/p3f/{flythrough,gpu_outdoor}` (at `0c88a0c3`),
`live/bin/p3f2/flythrough` (at `1b744e32`); archive `live/p3f-tests.tar.zst` (at `0c88a0c3`).

### 3. The test

`waits_parts` (`bake/queue/split_tests/thread.rs`): a hook sleeps 50 ms in the step before
the dig's; the assertions are the parts' sum within 0.5 ms of `laps[5]` (taken by
`render_frame`'s own marks, not the instants the parts use), the rest between the hold
less the send's offset less 0.5 ms and the hold plus 0.5 ms, and every other part under
the rest. The hook is given the queue (`Hook`: `&BakeQueue`, `&FrameMessage`), whose
`box_signal` reads a box sent and not taken (`BoxSignal::waits`); the sender's count rises
in `ThreadLink::send`, after the frame's `send` instant is taken.

## Phase 3g: the wait's parts tested by order, and the wait less its rest printed per frame

Commit `56117e85`. `DigWaitParts` keeps `span`, the wait from the send's instant to the
take's return (`DigWaitParts::of`), beside its six parts; `Frame::bake_frame_wait_span` reads
it (a new accessor: `bake_frame_wait_parts` keeps its shape, which `gpu_outdoor/dig_line.rs`
reads). `waits_parts`' hook no longer sleeps: on the held step's message it spins
(`yield_now`) until the queue's `box_signal` reads a box sent (`BoxSignal::waits`), and
panics after one second ("the gate: no box sent within 1 s of the held step"), which the
edit's frame returns as the thread's error. The fly-through's `bake thread dig wait:` line
gains `less rest p50/p99 A/B ms` after the wait's p50/p99/max: per dig frame its `span` less
its own `rest` (part 0), then the p50 and p99 over the state's dig frames
(`DigFrame::span`, NaN in lockstep). No line of the product's path changed but the kept
span. CPU only; no lock taken.

## Phase 4d: two lines and one batch of runs

Commit `cde2e966`. No mechanism. `QueueStats::submits` (the submits a step planned, 0 or 1,
summed over a frame's steps by `after`, zeroed by `idle`) and `QueueFed` (`stats.rs`): over the
frames pushed, fed = the samples planned into submits (`submitted`) over the caps granted
(`cap`, the newest step's; a frame with no step keeps it), submits a frame, and drawn a submit
= the samples planned over the submits times the mean cap (so fed is their product). The
fly-through pushes every frame whose stats read `draining` (the state the `queue split` line
files by) and prints `frames: queue fed: while in view waits ...`. The cycle: `Snapshot` keeps
its start (`at`); `ThreadLink` holds the newest step's `StepTimes` and, once taken, its books'
start and end, and closes the cycle (`Cycle`, `link.rs`) at the next step's `picked.free`. The
parts are wait (free to took), step (took to the step's return), snapshot (at to end) and
hand-over (the step's return to `at`, plus `end` to the next free: both outbox puts). The
cycles a take closed are `Frame::bake_thread_cycles`; the fly-through files those closed in
frames whose stats read `draining`, beside those frames' wall (the step-to-step interval), and
prints `frames: bake thread cycle: while in view waits N steps over M frames (S a frame),

### Deviations

- Files outside the list: `crates/svet-render/src/bake/queue/books.rs` (`after` sums and `idle`
  zeroes the new `submits`), `bake/queue/step.rs` (sets it from the step's `sent`),
  `bake/queue/mod.rs` and `lib.rs` (the exports of `QueueFed` and `CycleLine`),
  `bake/queue/split_tests/thread.rs` (`mod cycle;`) and the new
  `split_tests/thread/cycle.rs`, done-when (2)'s test (`thread.rs` is at 1 169 lines, the size
  cap 1 200). `apps/flythrough/src/run/frame.rs` is on the list and untouched.
- The fed line's submits are those the steps planned (`QueueStats::submits`), not those the
  frame issued (a frame issues at most one and carries the rest).
- The cycle line's frame wall is the interval between the fly-through's queue steps on those
  frames (the frame's wall), and its steps are the cycles that closed in them; a cycle closes
  when the next step's results are taken, so a cycle that spans the drain's end is filed by the
  frame that took the next step.
- The RENOIR forest's queue did not empty within its 6 000 frames (122.6 s); the empty column
  reads it so.

  key the score with no refine factor; under `Refine::Weight(r)` the lead is 0 and the key is
  `queue_score` with `r` for a coarse block. `queue_score` is the distance times `1 + angle x
  a`, times `r` if coarse; `angle_term` is 0 in the widened cone and the near shell and at
  most (pi less the cone) over pi behind. The defaults: `QUEUE_ANGLE_WEIGHT` 4,
  `QueueOrder::default()` the tier (`cli/parse.rs`: `queue_tier: true`).

  against 0.76 in the streamed cave, and the class was kept as the leading key.

## Phase 6a: the sleep-paced test, gated

Commit `9b39dc40`. The fading-chunk case (`a_dig_into_a_fading_chunk_shows_its_held_block`)
moves to `crates/svet-render/src/bake/queue/split_tests/thread/fade.rs` (`thread.rs` was at
1 169 lines; it is at 1 096). Its hook (`gated`) counts every step it enters and the newest
message frame among them (the count first), and holds the step of the message it names on
the queue's box signal (`BoxSignal::waits`), as Phase 3g's `waits_parts` does, with the same
one-second guard. Every frame from the first dig to the held step is `frame_settled`: the
frame, then a wait (up to a second, else the test fails) until its frame is entered and the
thread's step count equals the entered count, so each frame steps once at its own number and
the fade (paced by the frame number) moves one frame a step; the 1 ms sleep a frame and the
release thread's 50 ms sleep are gone. The assertions are unchanged; the case prints the held
step's frame, the edit's frame and the writes at chunk 0's place.

The main case (`the_edits_frame_waits_for_its_list_and_no_other_frame_does`) keeps its
100 ms release thread (its claim is that the box's frame returns only after a release the
test makes later), but its wait's instant is now taken before the thread is spawned (it was
taken after, so a main thread descheduled between the two could read under 0.1 s with the
order right), and the thread returns its release instant, which is asserted at or before the
frame's return. `waited >= 0.1` stands as written.

once it runs tests the lib binary run by name (`--exact`) 40 times, each under the memory cap.

| 02:52:51 (`live/p6a-40b.out`) | 6.47 / 12.96 | **40 passed, 0 failed**, 40 started while the tier ran | 614 run, 614 passed, 24 skipped |

Every run wrote 2 writes at chunk 0's place (the bar is 2 or more), the edit's frame two

`node scripts/test-changed.mjs --clippy` (flythrough, svet-render, svet-tester): clean.

against the 180 s line; fed 0.999, in view 12.7 s, share 0.322 / 0.527, wall p50 23.94 ms.

### The pins

`docs/nfr.md` section 2, the table "The bake step off the render thread", and its readings
paragraph at the end of `docs/nfr/02-frame-budget.md`: the share (met, both), the wall while in
view waits (met, both), fed (**RTX missed**, forest 0.611 and 0.599, hills 0.589 and 0.582, with
submits a frame and drawn a submit; RENOIR 1.000, 0.999, 0.996), in view (**RTX forest
missed**, 17.9 and 19.0 s, its two holders; hills 6.5 and 6.6 met; RENOIR 12.7, 12.7 and 11.5
met), the residency (met: RTX 26.2, 29.1, 23.2, 23.5 s; RENOIR 124.9 and 92.6), the outbox and
the shadow (**not read**: no line prints either; the outbox is bounded by construction at 8 MiB).
The dig's frame, the edit's wait and ring 1 are left to 6d.

### The Budgets rows

| row | standing |
|---|---|
| the render thread's bake share | read, both adapters (pinned) |
| the Floor frame while in view waits | read, both (pinned) |
| in-view drain | read, both (pinned) |
| whole-residency drain | read, both (pinned; the RENOIR forest by the fifth run) |
| the submits' drawn share | read, both (pinned); `second:150,0.25` on the RTX above |
| an edit's light | 6d's |
| memory | not read: no line at `cde2e966` prints the outbox's or the books' snapshot's bytes |
| the floor's CPU | the `taskset` arm above, the RTX only (the row names the stand-in on the RTX run) |
| images | a property, asserted by `gpu_queue` and `gpu_golden`: 6c's gate |
| section 7, the gate | 6c's |

`node scripts/check-*.mjs` (all six): exit 0.

### Deviations

- A fifth run (`renoir-forest-long`) beside the four: the done-when asks every Budgets row read
  on both adapters, and Phase 4d's RENOIR forest run ended before its queue emptied.
- The `taskset` arm has an unpinned companion run with the same thread sampler, so the runnable
  threads and the bake thread's busy share have a beside; its other figures repeat Phase 4d's.
- The withdrawn samples are derived from the timeline's rows (drawn less kept a read-back), not
  the `dig_withdrawn_samples` counter, which the rig does not print.
